"No way, Kitty! I won't let you do this on your own! We're both in this and so we're both going to confess together and probably get yelled at! So let's just get it over with right away and hopefully still be able to have that nap afterwards! Deal?"

She looked expectantly from one to the other, waiting for an answer.

Nathalie reacted first, her eyes narrowing, watching the girl up and down, having a sudden hunch.

"Why don't you go and sit in the dining room already? I'll go and get Gabriel and then we can talk. There's still some coffee, if you like, because you really seem to be struggling to keep your eyes open", she said to her, making her shake her head.

"I'm fine, thank you, Nathalie!", the teenager sighed wearily, to what the visibly pregnant woman turned towards her stepson and pleaded:

"Would you do me the favour and get your father for me? I would like to sit down with your girlfriend already, yeah?"

Adrien looked instantly worried and asked:

"Are you alright, Nathalie? If you're not feeling well..."

"I'm fine, just carrying a bit of extra weight and I'm a bit older than you, so if you don't mind..."

"Oh, of course not, I'm already on my way!"

"I can't believe, he bought that!", Marinette laughed the moment he had left, then she looked nervously at Nathalie, stating, "so....uhm....you figured it out, didn't you? What gave it away?"

Now the older woman scoffed:

"First of all, you never argue! Second, you never leave the house without having at least two large mugs of coffee.And third, even though you struggle every day, to get out of bed, you are way too energetic to take an afternoon nap. What could he possibly be aware of, that pointed towards a pregnancy, that you weren't? But I am thinking correctly, when I say you're pregnant, right?"

The girl shrugged while she walked next to the other woman into the huge dining room, where they both took a seat in front of the fireplace.

"I was angry at him, because he was aware that it had been eight weeks since the 'end' of my last period and he didn't even mention that once! "So, I guess, you haven't been at an examination yet, right?", the older woman continued and the girl shook her head. "Would you, I don't know, like me to make an appointment for you with my doctor or rather tell your parents first and go with your mum or completely on your own, maybe with Adrien?"

Marinette buried her face in her hands and groaned in despair.

"I have no idea!", she then exclaimed, "I just know, that I need to keep this from my dad as long as possible! After the way he reacted, when Adrien was just sleeping in my bed. Is your doctor nice? I mean, I am only seventeen and I wouldn't liked to be judged. Even though I am slowly getting used to the thought of becoming a mum and ruling the other options out completely!"

The teenager cupped her mouth with her hand, to not laugh out loud, then hissed: "Oh my

God, Nathalie! I love how you just spoke out, what none of us has dared to yet!"

The new Mrs Agreste laughed along with her. Then she gave her a stern look.

"Seriously, Adrien really tries to keep all his promises, but if 'you', in any way, feel uncomfortable with the whole situation, then don't let him push you into this direction! Okay. And you can tell me without being scared, I would tell him, what we were talking about. Sometimes we need someone to pour our heart, before we can go on with our everyday crap!"

The girl giggled again.

"I really love the way, you are today. But honestly, I never wanted to have an abortion, but, I didn't want to start a family that early either, so, I had to at least consider every option, before I could make a decision. I am well aware, that Adrien did feel differently from the very beginning. He is looking forward to it, while I am still struggling with the situation itself. I am not completely at a point, where I can accept it, though I am sure, I will get there by the end of this day. Because, if we are telling you and Gabriel, we sadly need to tell my parents too and that means, my father will start yelling and of course I will immediately stand up against him and take Adrien's side and our future child's..... Well, yeah, so....as I said, Adrien is really happy, and I am, let me say, I can handle the thought!", she reluctantly admitted to her actual feelings.

Nathalie was about to answer to that, when the door opened and Adrien and Gabriel entered the room. While the younger one just sat on the armrest of his girlfriend's chair, Gabriel pulled one closer to the one, his wife was sitting in, instantly reaching for her hand and leaning towards her, to kiss her cheek. The pregnant woman though, wasn't content with just a peck and pulled him closer by his collar, to entangle him into a prolonged, savage kiss, making him take a sharp breath through his nose, while he squeezed her hand a little tighter. With a deep sigh she eventually pulled back and leaned her back against the leather of the armchair, placing one hand on her belly.

It was his wife though, who took the word first:

"To be completely honest, darling, I already know, what they wanted to tell us, so.....uhm.... will you do me a tiny favour?"

"You know, that I would do everything, you asked from me, right, my love?", he cooed and kissed the knuckles of her hand. "So just tell me, what kind of favour, do you need from me?"

"Just the promise, that you will neither freak out, nor start yelling!"

"What did they do?", Gabriel now sighed.

"Promise me to not yell first!", Nathalie demanded.

"Fine, I promise!"

"Thank you! Now, Adrien, it's your turn, tell your father the news!"

"News?", the designer questioned, "at least that doesn't sound as if you broke something expensive or did another stupid thing, that could get you into trouble, like stealing or vandalism. So, it's probably something you want to do, that I won't approve of. What is it? A trip? A special school? Taking a break after school? Quitting school?"

Adrien cleared his throat, then began to stutter:

"A-Actually, i-it's probably a t-t-tiny bit of all of th-that! It's gonna b-be one hell of a t-t-trip, I think, not a s-special school, b-but, kinda t-taking a b-b-break. A-A-And at least I w-will quit my education f-f-for some time t-t-to t-t-take c-c-care of....!"

"Please calm down, G-Gabriel!", Nathalie hummed and started to caress the back of his hand with her thumb.

He turned his head angrily towards her, but the moment he looked into her eyes, looked into the face of the woman, he loved so very much, he felt calmer right away and nodded.

"Sorry, my love! Go on, please, Adrien!"

The young man took a deep breath and said with a firm voice:

"I am going to take a break after school, so that Marinette can finish her education, while I'll take care of our child!"

The young couple waited for the outburst, but it never came. The man was too overwhelmed by that, to even speak right now.

"Father? Can you please say something?", the model asked after a while.

Gabriel kept staring at him for several minutes, his mouth slightly open and they were already getting scared, that he might have a stroke or a heart attack or something, when he shook his head very slowly, with his eyes shut.

As he opened them again, they were brimming with tears.

"Come here, Adrien!", he ordered and the boy just did as he was told. Standing anxiously in front of his father, he waited for whatever would happen. He just wouldn't have expected the man to pull his son into a tight embrace, while the tears were streaming down his cheeks and he sobbed:

"I am really happy, that you found someone to be as comfortable with, that you can do that, after all you mother did to you!"

After they parted, the designer added:

"I will still yell and shout at both of you, for being so stupid and careless, just so you know. But I will also support you in everything and Nathalie and I will be here for you, no matter what. Come here, Marinette, let me hug you too. Do your parents know already?"

The young woman shook her head and Gabriel could see the fear in her eyes, when he said: "Don't worry about telling your dad, I will come with you and make sure, that the man stays calm and won't try to hurt anyone or cut something off of someone!"

Chapter 23: You'll Have To Make A Choice

"Do we really have to go to school today?", Marinette lamented, when Adrien tried to shake her awake, "I thought it was already bad enough, that we're going to tell my parents in the evening! Not to forget the appointment with the doctor in our lunch break!"

He chuckled and pulled away the blanket, while he explained:

"We have to! Your parents will only react worse, when you're not attending your classes. And as long as Miss Bustier isn't there, we won't find someone who we can talk to about it and who would be understanding. Except you would like to try Miss Mendeleiev or principal Damokles!"

She slowly pushed herself into a sitting position, her hair standing into all directions, her eyes still shut, making her boyfriend laugh at her.

"Come on, Bugaboo! It took me so long to get you to wake up, that we don't have much time left. You need to get ready!"

"You should better clear the way, Adrien, because....."

She couldn't say anything else because she had smashed both hands to her mouth, and was now jumping out of the bed and running past him and into the bathroom, slamming the door shut behind her, before she emptied her stomach contents into the toilet.

The young man sighed and followed her slowly, gently knocking, when he heard the sounds of her puking stop.

He opened the door and entered with a laugh. But a short glimpse at her face made him immediately stop.

Even breakfast couldn't help much with Marinette's mood this morning and so they both sat down in their seats in class, Adrien as usual in front of Marinette, none of them really listening to what Miss Mendeleiev was talking about.

It was thanks to Alya and Nino, who kept nudging their elbows into their friend's sides, that they didn't get detention that day and both were relieved, when the bell rang for the daily lunch break.

The former assistant squeezed the girl's hand, the moment she sat down beside her and said softly:

"I thought you were looking a little lost, when we were having breakfast, so I decided, to come with you. If it's okay for the both of you!"

She gave Adrien a questioning look.

The young man nodded vigorously.

"Whatever makes Marinette feel better and safer, will be okay with me, Nath. Thank you a lot!"

They had decided, to wait until after the visit at the doctor's, before talking to the woman's parents, which meant, a whole week had passed since telling Gabriel and her.

"Mrs Agreste! Is something wrong? Aren't you feeling well?", the doctor asked worriedly, when he saw the pregnant woman enter the examination room. Only as she shook her head, he noticed the teenagers following right behind her.

"This isn't my appointment today, but my son's girlfriend's!", she explained.

Then she told him about the situation and that Marinette's parents would probably not be very happy about it, while she and Gabriel were supporting them in everything. After she finished, the gynaecologist turned to the young woman and asked: "Well, Marinette, can I call you by your first name?"

She nodded with a shy smile.

"Good. Well, Marinette, when was you last period?"

The teenagers looked at each other sheepishly, making the doctor pry:

"What's the problem? Don't you know? We can also tell from the examination, how far along you approximately are!"

"Well, I can tell you, that her last period ended about nine and a half weeks ago!"

"No, we just had to deal with a lot of things, so only Adrien noticed and he thought, I was aware of it and as I didn't find it strange, he didn't give it too much thought either! At least not until a few days ago, when he was teasing me with something and then had to prove his point by showing me off!"

The young physician had watched them bickering for a moment, but interjected now:

"About five!", the girl muttered, feeling instantly ashamed for not noticing at all.

The couple nodded.

"Well, that would make you a little more than ten weeks pregnant already. Meaning you would be in your eleventh week, if the calculations were correct. I can tell you more after the ultrasound examination, which might feel a little unpleasant, but it will only last for a short time, until I find the embryo. Amazed by the tiny creature, which already had small arms and legs attached to its body and really assembled a human being already, even having a beating heart, Marinette could barely hold back the tears, when she squeezed Adrien's hand firmly, making him wince even. Meaning, I will spoil you with everything you wish for, so you will be distracted all the time before your parents arrive for dinner. How's that?", he proposed and made her smile wearily.

this will work. Let's just hope so!"

Tom and Sabine arrived at the Agreste mansion a couple of minutes before 7.30 PM. They had just as every day closed the bakery at seven to clean up the food display and the rest of the shop, while the bakery itself, with the oven and the kneading machine and all the other things, had already been scrubbed or washed in the morning, when the massive baker had finished his daily amount and variety of baked goods.

The couple had been surprised by the sudden invitation of Mr and Mrs Agreste, but Sabine had quickly found a to her logical explanation. The boy's father had married again not too long ago and he and his wife were expecting their first common child. While during the first months of a pregnancy, not only nausea but also an incredibly strong fatigue was bothering the most women, in the second trimester most of them were feeling rather good and so, now was the perfect time, to meet the parents of their son's girlfriend. Later there would be too much to organise or even already a newborn baby, claiming most of their time and attention for the first weeks.

"Well, the food was very delicious, but I can't get rid of the feeling, that there is a reason, why you invited us. Did our daughter do anything stupid? Because, before you judge her you must know, that she is incredibly clumsy and most likely didn't do it on purpose...!"

"I am very certain, that it wasn't on purpose!" Marinette

gasped, but joined her laughter.

"Later, Kitty!", whispered his girlfriend and he grinned a little dumbly and nodded.

She blindly felt for Adrien's hand and the moment he met hers, he could feel her tremble.

Interlinking their fingers, she pulled a folded piece of paper from her purse and pushed it along the table over to her mother.

Sabine narrowed her brows, then picked it up and following an instinct, she blocked her husband's view when she unfolded it. Her mouth fell open and her eyes widened, but she managed, to keep every other reaction contained, and as she had luckily turned away from Tom, he couldn't look at her face either.

"Why are you making a secret of it, Sabine? I am certain, she wanted us both to look at, whatever it is!"

"Yeah, that's what I thought. You can go into Gabriel's office, if you want some quiet and private space. Marinette knows the way!"

he snarled.

The baker scoffed.

"You don't think so? YOU DON'T THINK SO? Well, Marinette, you'll have to make a choice then! Your family or your lover?", he bellowed.

"Tom!", Sabine pleaded, but he shook his head.

"So? I'm waiting for your answer!"

The young woman straightened herself after getting up too, then she looked directly into her father's eyes:

"You are wrong in more than one thing, dad. Because, you want me to decide between the family I come from and the family, that lies ahead of me. And you can't really even think for the split of a second, I could make a decision for you and the way you're acting just now and against the tiny human, growing inside of me and the man I have been in love with since the day we met! If you want to leave, leave! I made my decision even before I saw what's on the picture you're holding and before we I heard the little heart beating. I hope, we can meet sometimes, mum? If not, we can talk over the phone, yeah? But right now, I can't continue this, because I shouldn't get all worked up even more. So please, just leave. If there is nothing nice, you can say, just go!"

The furious man wanted to yell at the girl, when Gabriel, who had stayed quiet until now, rose from his chair and approached him. Despite being only half his weight, the designer's height itself had an impact on people and the baker was silenced in an instant.

"I really think it best, if you were to leave now, because I won't let you yell and accuse all evening, while there are two pregnant women in the room, for who that much of excitement won't do any good. So, as long as you can't stay calm, I ask you to leave my house. Good night!"

With that, he turned his back to the man and walked to his wife and the young couple, laying his arms protectively around both females, while giving his son a sign of approval and support with his head.

Tom just grabbed Sabine's arm and dragged her fumingly outside the mansion.

As soon as they were gone, Marinette buried her face in the crook of Adrien's neck and sobbed desperately. The young model just held her taut and rubbed her back, sending an helpless glare at his father and Nathalie.

The adults just shrugged and Gabriel said softly:

"Just take her to your room and let her cry. It's best, to let it all out!", he then spoke directly to the half chinese girl, "don't worry, Marinette, we're here for you and neither of us will let you down! You'll see, everything will be alright!"

Chapter 24: How Would You Even Dare?

A couple of weeks had passed and Marinette still hadn't recovered from their loss. Barely two days after the dinner with her parents, she had woken up in the middle of the night to a strong pain in her lower abdomen and a massive bleeding.

After being rushed into the hospital, the doctors could just tell her the sad news though, that she was just having a miscarriage. After going under a small surgery and having to stay in the clinic for another day, Adrien was allowed to take her home with him and try to be there for his girlfriend.

At first, she had tried to be strong, to not show, how much it hurt her. Had told him, that they were far too young anyway. The young man had just held her hand and kissed the back of it, not able to speak at all. But he had agreed to everything, she said, so she could cope with the situation in her very own pace and way.

Then, when she had woken after the surgery back in her hospital room, she had completely broken down.

Crying angrily, shouting at him, at the nurses and even at the doctor who came in to check on her. Then Marinette had desperately clung to his neck, sobbing violently into his shoulder, while he kept rocking her carefully, to not worsen the pain from the operation.

Eventually the young woman had fallen asleep, a light and obviously troubled sleep, as she was shifting and fidgeting on the bed. It was already noon by then, and while the teenager watched the girl, he had been in love with for so long, feeling guilty for getting her into this situation, feeling numb, because he had tried to not show any emotion at all, so he could be her rock, about to start crying too, he felt a hand on his shoulder and as he turned around, he could see Gabriel standing there, his eyes puffy, his expression painfully distraught.

"Just because I love this little guy so very much already! Though I am certain, that he loves me just as much and would gladly wait until you made me feel really good!", she whined.

"Come on! After you had something to eat, you'll have a lot more strength and can endure multiple 'good feelings', and won't have to tell me, you can't stand one more orgasm right now, as you did last time!"

The girl rolled her eyes as she took his hands and let him pull her up to then vanish into the bathroom to empty her all the time full bladder.

When they arrived in the dining room, Gabriel sat already in his usual spot, while Nathalie was still walking up and down the table patting her daughter's back repeatedly to get her to burp.

Marinette's mood instantly changed and she beamed widely and held out her hands.

"Hello my little sweetheart. Come to your sister in law and let your mummy eat already. I need a moment to work up an appetite, so....", she cooed and, without letting the older female object, took the baby from her.

"Is something wrong?", Nathalie asked and gave her a stern look.

But the pregnant woman shook her head.

"I'm fine. I just woke up a couple of minutes ago and lately I need a bit of time between getting up and being able to eat. If I tried now, it would come back in an instant, even if I am feeling very well just now!"

The other woman nodded understandingly and patted the girl's shoulder.

"I remember that feeling. Just take your time. And thank you for taking care of Belle in the meantime!"

The younger female gave her an encouraging tilt with her head before she, just like Nathalie before, began to pace through the room, gently rocking the little girl while patting onto her back.

She soon let out an audible burp and spat a bit of milk onto the woman's shoulder.

"Stay where you are!", Marinette called out to Nathalie who had already put down the cutlery and was about to get up and help with that issue. "Just finish your dinner, we'll just wipe that off quickly and then I certainly can eat something too. Right my 'belle amie'?"

The others who sat around the table chuckled and while the older ones continued to eat, Adrien watched his wife dreamily.

"....ien!"

He flinched and looked frantically around, not sure, where his name had come from.

"Finally!", the half Chinese laughed and placed his sister in the bend of his elbow. "Belle wanted to cuddle with her big brother while her sister in law would like to take in some food! Where have you been with your thoughts, Kitty?"

Adrien blinked a couple of times as he took a firm hold of the three months old and grinned sheepishly.

"I was watching my beautiful wife and my baby sister and began to think about the future and how it's going to be, once our own baby will be here. It's going to be so amazing to see you with our son very soon, Bugaboo!", he admitted.

"I love you!", was her only response to that.

With a beaming smile he focused on the baby in his arms, and gave his wife the time to fill her plate and start to eat, before he would tell her that Kagami sent her regards. He however wouldn't even get the chance to do so, all of them being fully unaware of what was about to happen. He had just placed Belle back in her mother's arms, when they heard some noise from the outside, like somebody trying to force the iron gate open.

And less than a minute after they had noticed the first sounds, that was exactly, what happened. A huge vehicle, some sort of tractor or some bulldozer from a construction side, broke through the two winged gate and made it fall to the ground with a deafening thud.

Having ignored the first noise, thinking it most likely coming from a car bumping into another vehicle, they now were startled, and the men abruptly jumped up from their chairs and rushed to the nearest window, while Marinette and Nathalie stayed in their seats, the latter scooting a little closer to the girl to place a hand on her forearm to keep her calm.

"Wh-what was that?" came from the younger woman.

Nathalie shrugged but looked expectantly towards Adrien and Gabriel, hoping they would be able to figure it out very soon. But as soon as they reached the end of the large dining room and gazed outside they both turned deeply red, the veins on their foreheads and necks swelling and becoming visible.

"This is it! I'm just going to kill him!" blurted the young man out and was already about to transform, when he felt his father's hand on his shoulder.

"Wait, Adrien! Don't be hasty now and make the worst mistake in your life! He's not worth it! I don't know how on earth he got here, but if you tried to transform into ChatNoir now you would expose yourself to any sort of danger, because your anger would let everybody figure out your real identity in an instant!" Gabriel pleaded sternly.

"Who are you talking about?" his wife wondered.

The answer came from a very pale Marinette. Albeit her voice was steady and cold as she contemptuously spat, "My so called father!"

Nathalie gasped.

"How did he get out of the prison and made it all the way here? What's he going to do?"

"If I had to guess, I'd say he wants to kill us both this time to pay me back for exposing him. But he won't get very far with that, right, Tikki? I need you to get me the bee miraculous very quickly and give it to Gabriel! I am sure, you'll know what to do with it, right?" Marinette addressed her father in law.

Gabriel nodded.

"Even without having deciphered the grimoire I would have known which power destroyed which of my plans!" he smirked. "Just hurry, he's approaching the entrance doors!"

Giving her kwami a tilt with her head the young guardian sent her off to get the demanded jewel.

Tikki soared straight through the ceiling and returned only a minute later on the regular way, as she couldn't take the miraculous through the concrete and iron and all the other things the mansion was built of, with her.

Eying the little comb for the split of a second pensively Gabriel then pushed it into his hair right above his left ear and as soon as the little bee appeared he called out,

"Pollen, buzz on!"

He knew, he looked ridiculous wearing a yellow and black striped spandex suit and long antennae with fluffy, little balls on their ends. But he had tried to look as harmless as possible to not scare his little daughter, no matter if she could see him or not at her age.

Ignoring how pathetic he was feeling, he took position on the first floor behind the reiling, just above the entrance and said calmly,

"Venom!"

Then he just waited, his weapon aiming at the spot where he expected the baker to enter. "Nathalie, before he destroys this door too, be so kind and push the button to open it, yeah? I would like to let him walk with open eyes into the trap! He'll certainly think, we will surrender and beg for our lives and feel triumphant and careless, when he comes inside!" Gabriel whisper-yelled to his wife.

The young mother who was holding her daughter close to her chest, reacted quickly and did as she was asked to do.

Just as Gabriel had predicted, the despicable man stomped with his head held high and a contemptuous expression into the mansion.

"Marinette!" he called out with a lilting, vicious voice. "No use in trying to give in to anything now. I am here to end it once and for all. You should have just chosen your family instead of that bastard you were carrying! I am here to make things right and end you and your new bastard child! So stop hiding behind your boyfriend's rich father's defences and show yourself so we can get it over with!"

"Actually," Adrien now said as he stepped in front of Tom Dupain, "it's my wife you're talking about. So this is 'your' last chance to leave the premises or you won't see it coming, Tom!"

The dark haired man who was still wearing the prison suit, cackled.

"And who is going to stop me? You? Do you think, I came unprepared?"

While speaking he pulled a gun from the hem of his pants but at the same moment he aimed it at the teenager, he was hit by the spinning top Gabriel had let go at him and froze in the spot.

While they let the Gorilla tie him up and hand him over to the police, Nathalie had called in the meantime, both couples hugged each other in relief and laughed happily that the nightmare had finally ended and Tom would be locked up better and for much longer in the future, maybe even forever due to his mental state.

"It's finally over! Now we can look forward to a beautiful future for all of us!" Adrien exclaimed later that night as he raised his glass to celebrate their triumph. "Despite just having non-alcoholic-champagne, I want to drink to all our future!"

"To all our future!" all the people surrounding him and his wife called out.

Sabine had cheered along with all of them, but then she had become very quiet and waited for the turmoil to calm down a little bit.

When the moment seemed right, she reluctantly cleared her throat. The last voices stopped in an instant and everyone looked at her expectantly.

"I am not sure how to say this without sounding stupid or self-centered. But I really feel as if this was the right moment to tell you all about the things in my life, that have changed recently. It was really tough, getting over the things, Tom did. I know, it was a thousand times worse for you, sweetheart, but for me it was a hard time too. I lost my husband, my grandchild and almost my daughter or at least her trust. Over all this time there was one person, who helped me through all of that and gave me strength and..." She paused and looked at the people around her, then shyly grasped her best friend's, Nadja Chamack's, hand. "And what none of us had expected, we both found ourselves attracted to each other and this attraction soon turned into more. We...well, we're in love and we will move in together next month. I hope, you can all be happy with us after the first shock... uhm... that's it, I think..." Beaming widely her daughter walked towards her and pulled her into her arms.

"There is no need to get over any shock, mum! We love you and want you to be happy! And honestly, Nathalie and I have a bet going, how much longer the two of you will keep your relationship a secret. Manon told us about you spending the nights together and though she let that drop all innocently, we instantly could put two and two together. I'm really happy for both of you!"

Marinette hugged Nadja now, as did Adrien and Nathalie, while Gabriel just shook both women's hands.

"Now all we are waiting for to make our luck complete, is for you to be born!" Adrien spoke against the skin of his wife's protruding belly.

"Yeah, that and a couple of siblings for him. And for Adrien and Belle!" Gabriel mused and winked at his own wife.

"Actually," Nathalie began hesitantly, while she gazed down at her hand, which was lying on her lower stomach.

She didn't have to say another word when Gabriel dropped to his knees in front of the armchair she was sitting in and cupped her belly with both hands while he started to kiss her passionately.

The End?
